### 2026-10-16 14:36:05  #其他

【三部門：對兜底保障型機構基本服務費實行政府指導價管理】三、《指導意見》的主要內容是什麼？

答：《指導意見》的主要內容主要包括：一是清晰界定各型別養老機構範圍。兜底保障型機構是指完全或主要收住特困老年人和經濟困難失能老年人等群體的公辦養老機構。普惠支援型機構包括以收住社會老年人為主的公建公營和公建民營養老機構，以及接受場地費用減免或建設運營補貼等政府支援的社會辦養老機構。其他養老機構為完全市場型機構。二是分級分類完善養老服務價格管理方式。對兜底保障型機構基本服務費實行政府指導價管理，對特困老年人和經濟困難失能老年人按規定實行免費收住政策或給予相關補助，切實發揮保基本、兜底線、穩民生的基礎作用。對普惠支援型機構基本服務費，公建公營和公建民營機構實行政府指導價，社會辦機構可設定收費標準參考區間加以引導或實行政府指導價，讓政府投入的政策紅利真正傳導到群眾。對完全市場型機構收費實行市場調節價，鼓勵優質優價，滿足老年人多樣化需求。對醫養結合服務允許根據成本增加等情況，合理制定收費標準，調動機構特別是公立醫療機構提供醫養結合服務的積極性。對社群養老服務，全託床位在10張以上的按照養老機構管理，10張以下的原則上實行市場調節價，滿足老年人就近就便養老需求。對居家養老服務實行市場調節價管理，借鑑部分地方的有益經驗做法，可由行業主管部門牽頭明確上門服務費的收費標準參考區間，為服務機構自行定價提供指引。三是規範定價和收費行為。建立目錄清單管理制度，所有收費進清單，清單之外一律不得收費。明確收費標準制定原則，總體按照“補償成本、合理盈利”的原則確定政府指導價或收費標準參考區間。建立收費動態評估調整機制，評估週期原則上不超過3年。同時，注重價格政策與其他各項支援政策的協同聯動，共同支援相關養老服務模式發展。

---

【中國駐菲律賓大使回應菲防長涉華謬論：究竟誰才是麻煩製造者？】 10月15日，井泉大使出席第二屆迪利曼對話會期間接受菲律賓媒體聯合採訪。當被問及如何看待菲律賓國防部長特奧多羅將中國稱為本地區“麻煩製造者”的言論時，井大使作出如下回應：

“這種說法純屬無稽之談。究竟誰才是麻煩製造者？看看是誰在針對無辜的華文教師？是誰帶領軍隊逮捕無辜的中國工人？又是誰每天都在抹黑、攻擊中國？

這些針對中國的指責毫無根據，意在炒作虛假敘事，煽動兩國人民之間的仇恨。究竟是誰每天都在製造新問題、挑起新事端，試圖將雙方推向對抗？誰才是真正的麻煩製造者？

菲律賓應當感到慶幸，擁有中國這樣一個鄰居。如果我們要動用武力，完全有可能輕易取勝。但我們不會使用武力，因為我們熱愛和平。

本地區至少享有和平。看看世界其他地方——烏克蘭、加沙，甚至委內瑞拉，那裡正在發生什麼？我們應當感到慶幸，至少我們還享有和平。

我們希望本地區各國，特別是東亞和東南亞國家，能夠共同努力，珍視和平，謀求共同發展。中國願意與周邊國家分享發展經驗和技術，希望各國將中國的發展視為機遇，而不是威脅。”

TAIEX Rebounds from 4-Week Low but Posts Weekly Loss — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, rose 141 points, or 0.3%, to close at 47,111 on Friday, rebounding after falling in the previous session and moving away from its lowest level since September 17, touched a day earlier. The rebound tracked gains in Asian market peers, following an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight as the tech selloff eased. However, traders looked ahead to the release of a slew of Chinese data, including GDP growth, due next week. Most sectors traded in the green, including electronic technology, which gained 0.2% after plunging 4% on Thursday. MediaTek climbed 2.8%, Delta Electronics gained 0.3%, and Hon Hai Precision rose 2.7%. However, TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, fell 0.2% after CEO C.C. Wei warned on Thursday about macroeconomic uncertainty and weak consumer demand. For the week, the index slumped 4.6%, marking its first weekly decline in five weeks amid a tech selloff.

【印尼未來資產證券：MSCI市場分類調整提案存在長期風險】印尼未來資產證券表示，MSCI提議將印尼從新興市場重新劃為獨立市場，是一項“重大事態”，可能對該國本土資本市場產生長期影響。研究主管兼首席經濟學家魯利·阿里亞·維斯努布羅託在一份研報中寫道，從長期來看，此次調整會壓縮印尼的機構投資者群體、降低市場流動性，並抬升長期風險溢價。“其影響範圍可能比我們當下看到的拋售壓力更為深遠。”一旦市場分類調整落地，跟蹤新興市場指數的被動投資者或將拋售印尼股票，主動型機構投資者也可能調整持倉，進而引發外資流出。該券商建議分步佈局，優先選擇外資機構持股比例較低、受MSCI相關拋售壓力影響更小的個股，部分大宗商品類股票值得關注。

Swedish Jobless Rate Falls to 8.1% — Sweden’s unemployment rate fell to 8.1% in September 2026 from 8.3% in the same period a year earlier. The total number of unemployed people declined by 9.5 thousand from September 2025 to 466.4 thousand, which consists of 239 thousand unemployed women and 227 thousand unemployed men, corresponding to 8.6% and 7.6%, respectively. Among people aged 15-24, 168 thousand were unemployed, corresponding to a youth unemployment rate of 20.8%. The number of long-term unemployed, defined as those jobless for at least 27 weeks, amounted to 173 thousand, with women accounting for 74 thousand and men 99 thousand. Meanwhile, employment among people aged 15-74 rose by 54.2 thousand from a year ago to 5.3 million, including 2.53 million women and 2.76 million men. The average total number of hours worked per week stood at 175.4 million. On a seasonally adjusted basis, the unemployment rate was 8.7%, while the youth unemployment stood at 23.2%.

### 2026-10-16 14:04:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

ASX 200 Marks 3rd Straight Weekly Rise, Up 1% — The ASX 200 jumped 90 points, or 1.0%, to finish at 8,807 on Friday, reaching a near five-week high that snapped two days of declines. Stronger U.S. stock futures lifted sentiment as traders rotated out of major tech names into broader sectors. Locally, softer-than-expected labor data in Australia cooled bets on a near-term hike by the Reserve Bank. However, the upside was limited by growing caution ahead of Chinese September activity and Q3 GDP data due next week. Gains were broad, led by tech, healthcare, manufacturing, and commercial services. Heavyweights BHP jumped 3.5% after management and the supervisors' union at the Escondida copper mine in Chile reached a last-minute deal earlier this week, avoiding a strike at the world's largest copper. Gold miners also advanced, boosted by Northern Star Resources (3.3%) and Evolution Mining (3.0%). Other standouts included South32 (3.6%), REA Group (3.1%), and CSL (2.8%). Markets rose 1% for the week, the third straight weekly gain.

### 2026-10-16 14:01:45  #央行 #宏觀 #焦點

【藍佛安會見歐盟委員會經濟和生產力委員東布羅夫斯基斯、歐洲中央銀行行長拉加德和歐元集團主席皮埃拉卡基斯】2026年10月16日，財政部部長藍佛安在泰國曼谷出席世界銀行/國際貨幣基金組織年會和二十國集團（G20）財長和央行行長會議期間應約與歐盟委員會經濟和生產力委員東布羅夫斯基斯、歐洲中央銀行行長拉加德和歐元集團主席皮埃拉卡基斯舉行會談，就中歐宏觀經濟政策等議題進行了交流。財政部副部長廖岷陪同參加。 藍佛安表示，當前世界經濟面臨多重挑戰，中歐作為主要經濟體應推動各方踐行真正的多邊主義，反對各種形式的單邊主義和保護主義，加強宏觀政策協調，維護國際產供鏈穩定，為全球經濟增長注入動力。 藍佛安強調，全球失衡是複雜的經濟結構性問題，其成因源於經濟全球化發展和國際分工、主要貨幣主導地位、各國宏觀政策取向等多重因素，近年來還更多受到地緣政治、保護主義等非經濟因素影響。各方應全面、客觀、平衡看待失衡問題，透過開放合作與宏觀政策協調，推動全球經濟平衡、可持續增長。中國正在加快構建以國內大迴圈為主體、國內國際雙迴圈相互促進的新發展格局，擴大內需是重要戰略支點。“十五五”時期，中國將更加註重擴大內需，更加註重投資於人，更加註重開放共享，推動經濟實現質的有效提升和量的合理增長，持續為世界經濟增長提供機遇和重要支撐。中方願同歐方充分發揮中歐財金對話等機制作用，就完善全球經濟治理、加強宏觀政策協調等開展溝通。 (財政部)

【聚焦神經介入耗材，第八批耗材集採或將來臨】距離第七批國家高值醫用耗材集採開標一個月左右，神經介入耗材迎來資訊維護動作，市場對第八批耗材集採的預期升溫。10月15日晚間，天津市醫藥採購中心釋出《關於開展神經介入類醫用耗材產品資訊集中維護工作的通知》，定於10月16日至10月23日開展神經介入類醫用耗材產品資訊集中維護。本次擬納入品類覆蓋較為完整，基本涵蓋腦梗取栓、腦血管狹窄成形、腦動脈瘤栓塞等主流神經介入手術耗材，包含顱內支架、顱內取栓支架、彈簧圈、導引導管、微導絲等十餘個品類。產品資訊維護是集採報量、報價階段的前置基礎工作，此番維護被業內視作第八批神經介入耗材集採流程啟動的訊號。不過需要指出，最終採購範圍、採購規則仍以後續官方正式檔案為準。一位地方醫保部門工作人員日前向記者透露，心腦是兩個重要器官，心臟支架已經被納入國家集採，從節奏上看，眼下推進神經介入類耗材集採時機成熟。（21世紀經濟報道）

NZX 50 Ends 0.6% Lower, Little Changed for the Week — The NZX 50 dropped 79 points, or 0.6%, to close at 13,754 on Friday, halting gains from the morning session and the previous two sessions, mainly weighed down by losses in utilities, communication services, and real estate. The latest data weighed on sentiment, as food inflation hit a three-month high, while electronic card spending continued to fall in September. Traders also looked ahead to the release of Q3 inflation and September trade data, along with a slew of Chinese data, including GDP growth, due next week. However, accelerating growth in New Zealand's factory activity in August limited the decline. Meanwhile, an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight helped prevent a deeper fall as the tech selloff faded and Treasury yields eased. Among the notable decliners were Vulcan Steel (2.4%), Mainfreight (1.5%), Hallenstein Glasson (1.3%), AFT Pharmaceuticals (1.1%), and Infratil (1.0%). For the week, the index was almost flat after rising in the previous week.

### 2026-10-16 13:13:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 2

Indonesian Shares Suffer Biggest Daily Drop Since 1998 — Indonesian stocks plunged 630 points, or 10.8%, to around 5,233 by noon Friday, marking their steepest one-day decline since January 1998. Markets hit a six-year low following MSCI’s warning that it could downgrade Indonesia to standalone market status on persistent concerns over investability in Southeast Asia’s largest economy. The potential move threatens significant capital outflows, with Goldman Sachs previously estimating up to $13 billion in withdrawals. MSCI’s final decision will be due by June 30, 2027. Indonesia’s financial regulator said it was “carefully reviewing and paying serious attention to MSCI’s assessment." All major sectors recorded steep losses, led by basic materials, energy, financials, and infrastructure. The four largest banks tumbled between 8.5% and 14%, while Telkom Indonesia (-11.7%), Astra Intl. (-9.6%) and Jasa Marga (-14.8%) also posted deep losses. For the week, markets are on track to slump, down around 14% so far, reversing modest gains previously.

South Korean Won Extends Losses on Capital Outflows — The South Korean won traded around 1,343 per dollar, extending losses as foreign capital outflows and concerns over AI investments weighed on local markets. Foreign investors withdrew a net $19.97 billion from South Korean stocks and bonds in September, marking an eighth consecutive month of net outflows. The selling persisted into October, with foreign investors offloading KRW 3.6 trillion in local shares on Thursday amid heavy losses in Samsung Electronics and SK hynix, driven by concerns over AI valuations and Chinese competition in memory chips. Additionally, US producer prices rose 1.3% month-on-month in September, the largest increase since March 2022, while annual inflation accelerated to 6.3%, fueling concerns over persistent price pressures and potential Federal Reserve tightening. Meanwhile, a retreat in US Treasury yields and the dollar offered some external relief.

### 2026-10-16 12:14:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 2

Malaysia GDP Growth Slowest in a Year — Malaysia’s economy expanded 5.3% year-on-year in the third quarter of 2026, easing from 6% in the previous period and marking the slowest growth in a year, preliminary estimates showed. Mining and quarrying contracted by 4.9%, compared with 9.2% growth in Q2, reflecting lower crude oil and condensate production. Growth also moderated in services (5.4% vs 5.9%) and construction (6.0% vs 6.5%). In contrast, agriculture rebounded to 1.3% growth from a 3.7% contraction, driven by higher output in the oil palm and livestock subsectors. Manufacturing growth accelerated to 7.5% from 7.3%, supported by export-oriented industries, particularly electrical, electronic and optical products, as well as petroleum, chemical, rubber and plastic products. In the first three quarters of the year, Malaysia’s economy expanded by 5.5%, compared with 4.8% in the corresponding period a year earlier.

Copper Advances as Dollar Weakens — Copper futures climbed toward $6.60 per pound on Friday, snapping a three-session losing streak as the US dollar weakened amid concerns over a potential slowdown in the AI capital expenditure boom that has supported the American economy and financial markets. A softer dollar makes greenback-denominated commodities such as copper more affordable for overseas buyers, helping support demand. Prices also gained support from tightening supply, as labor strikes and adverse weather conditions disrupted mining operations in Chile, the world’s largest copper producer. Strikes affected BHP Group’s major Escondida mine and Antofagasta Plc’s Centinela mine, while unfavorable weather hampered open-pit operations at Codelco’s El Teniente mine. On the demand side, copper continued to benefit from strong growth prospects in data center infrastructure, semiconductor manufacturing, and battery energy storage systems.

### 2026-10-16 12:07:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Rallies on Supply Concerns, Eyes Second Weekly Gain — Malaysian palm oil futures surged more than 3% to above MYR 4,750 per tonne, erasing prior losses and hitting a three-week high. Firmer edible oils on China’s Dalian exchange and Chicago markets boosted sentiment, while supply concerns in top producer Indonesia also added support. The Indonesian Palm Oil Association projected output to fall to 51.7 million tonnes in 2027 from 53.7 million this year, with exports seen dropping nearly 15% yoy amid rising domestic biodiesel and food consumption. Meanwhile, cargo surveyors reported Malaysian shipments jumping 33.5%–37.7% in October 1–10 from the same period in September, signaling stronger near-term demand. Robust festival buying from India, the world's largest palm oil importer, further buoyed prices. However, record-high Malaysian inventories and growing caution ahead of Q3 GDP data next week in China, a major palm oil consumer, tempered gains. For the week, futures were on track for a second straight advance, up about 3.9% so far.

【Rail service numbers to be revised; new X prefix for EMUs above 350 km/h】China State Railway Group will adjust nationwide train numbers in two batches in late November and late December, adding a new X prefix for Fuxing EMUs with maximum operating speeds above 350 km/h. The X prefix (read “Xin”) will cover X1–X9998. 【China's first small-to-medium pumped-storage project under 15th Five-Year Plan enters commercial operation】On the 16th, CGN's Hubei Tuanfeng Weijiachong pumped-storage power station — the first small-to-medium pumped-storage project to enter operation during China's 15th Five-Year Plan — had its first unit begin commercial service. The station, in Tuanfeng county, Huanggang, Hubei, has total installed capacity of 298 MW, comprising two 149 MW reversible pump-turbine generator units. The facility, with upper and lower reservoirs and a water conveyance-generation system, will pump at grid off-peak and generate at peak, adding short-term grid flexibility and energy-storage capacity.

### 2026-10-16 11:25:50  #MKT News

【General Conference on Weights and Measures votes to end leap seconds】The 28th General Conference on Weights and Measures on the 15th in Versailles, France adopted a resolution to implement "continuous Coordinated Universal Time" from 20 May 2027. The change means UTC will no longer be adjusted by adding or subtracting leap seconds and could remain continuous for several centuries. UTC is currently the only time scale recommended as an international reference; it runs at the same rate as International Atomic Time while historically using leap seconds to stay close to Earth-rotation-based Universal Time. Since 1972, UTC has added or removed one second when the monitored difference reached 0.9s. Under the resolution the maximum permitted difference going forward is set at 3,600 seconds (one hour).

### 2026-10-16 11:18:20  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Prices Hover Near Peaks — European natural gas prices eased to €85.5 per megawatt-hour, hovering just below a multi-year high, as investors anxiously weigh the bloc’s supply outlook ahead of the heating season. EU gas storage facilities were 74.1% full, far lower than typical levels for this time of year, with some countries struggling to meet the minimum storage requirements. Replenishing inventories has become challenging for Europe amid continued shipping disruptions through the Strait of Hormuz, with Middle Eastern supplies remaining about 80% lower than a year earlier. This sets the stage for fiercer competition between Asia and Europe, the world’s two largest LNG-importing regions, for available cargoes as temperatures fall. Prices remain vulnerable to further spikes in the event of a colder winter and prolonged supply disruptions.

Taiwan Stocks Rebound from 4-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, rose 518 points, or 1.1%, to 47,488 around noon on Friday, rebounding after falling in the previous session and touching its lowest level since September 17, a day earlier. The rebound tracked gains in Asian market peers, following an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight as the tech selloff eased. However, high oil prices capped gains amid concerns over inflation and interest rate hikes. Traders also looked ahead to the release of a slew of Chinese data, including GDP growth, due next week. Most sectors traded in the green, including electronic technology, which rose 1.1%, after a 4% plunge on Thursday. MediaTek rose 3.1%, Delta Electronics gained 0.9%, and Hon Hai Precision climbed 2.1%. However, TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, declined 0.4% after CEO C.C. Wei warned on Thursday that consumer demand and price-sensitive end-market segments were facing challenges.

The index provider flagged dysfunction in local markets and outlined a possible shift to standalone-market classification, which could trigger capital outflows. The prospect added pressure on the currency amid persistent volatility. Meanwhile, Bank Indonesia will hold a policy meeting next week. The central bank has kept its key rate at 5.75% for three straight months through September, following cumulative hikes of 100bps in May and June, while continuing forex intervention to support the rupiah. September inflation accelerated to 3.28%, driven by persistent pressure from food prices. Globally, the dollar index eased but remained firm near 102, as resilient U.S. data reinforced expectations of further Fed tightening. For the week, the rupiah lost around 0.4%, reversing gains in the previous two periods

Japan 10-Year Yield Rises as BOJ Outlook Mulled — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield climbed to around 2.95% on Friday, recovering some ground after the previous session’s decline as investors assessed hawkish signals from a Bank of Japan official. BOJ board member Junko Koeda cautioned this week against excessive hesitation in raising interest rates, reinforcing expectations that the central bank could continue tightening monetary policy in the coming months following its accelerated pace of normalization. Earlier reports suggested that the BOJ was reluctant to deliver a rare back-to-back rate hike this month, although a potential increase in December remained under consideration. Meanwhile, BOJ Governor Kazuo Ueda said policymakers would continue to monitor bond yields closely, reiterating that long-term interest rates should be determined by market forces. His comments underscored the central bank’s measured approach as it continues to move away from its previous yield curve control framework.

【PBOC deputy governor Tao Ling says will continue to support offshore RMB lending and cross-border trade finance】PBOC Deputy Governor Tao Ling said on Oct. 16 at the Global Wealth Management Forum in Shanghai that the central bank will continue supporting financial institutions’ offshore RMB lending and cross-border trade finance to better meet offshore RMB liquidity needs. The PBOC will refine bank cross-border interbank RMB financing rules, encourage more offshore institutions to issue and trade panda bonds, and support Hong Kong’s dim sum bond market to broaden liquidity and risk-management tools. Building on existing offshore RMB bond repo and cross-border repo operations, it will push development of the repo and derivatives markets, product innovation and mechanism upgrades, support clearing banks’ liquidity-provision role in offshore markets, and use currency-swap mechanisms to establish regular swap-funding arrangements with additional eligible countries and regions.

【Yuehai Feed says very strong El Niño presents mixed impact】Yuehai Feed said at an Oct. 15 investor meeting that the WMO and US NOAA warned in Sept. 2026 the current El Niño is expected to peak by year-end at near-super-strong intensity and could persist into early 2027. The company called the event two-sided: a warmer South China winter may lengthen aquaculture cycles and raise demand and growth efficiency for shrimp and marine fish feed, and fewer northwest Pacific typhoons would lower storm risk in main production areas. Risks include abnormal water temperatures increasing disease and pond losses, extreme drought or flood damage to facilities, and El Niño-driven disruptions to global soybean and corn regions that could push up raw-material prices.

【MSCI proposes removing Indonesia from Emerging Markets index; analyst calls move too harsh】MSCI said Indonesia no longer meets Emerging Markets eligibility and proposed removing the country from its widely followed suite of indices, a change that could prompt sizable portfolio outflows. Angus Mackintosh, ASEAN markets specialist at Alethia Capital, called the move overly severe given steps Indonesia has taken to address market transparency and free-float, saying MSCI’s case rests on insufficient evidence those reforms are implemented and on a failure to sanction participants in apparent price manipulation. Mackintosh added Indonesia can only reverse the decision by imposing strict sanctions and fines on those involved in collusive manipulation, and that grouping Indonesia with markets such as Lebanon, Palestine and Argentina misrepresents the operating condition of most listed companies.

### 2026-10-16 10:25:26  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesian Stocks Sink 8% at Open on MSCI Downgrade Risk — Indonesian equities opened sharply lower Friday, tumbling 470 points or 8% to 5,393 after MSCI proposed downgrading the country to standalone market status, citing persistent governance concerns and suspected coordinated trading. A final decision is due June 30, 2027. Despite reforms, MSCI flagged weak enforcement and market dysfunction. The Financial Services Authority pledged continued improvements, stressing domestic markets remain attractive. Goldman Sachs earlier estimated a downgrade could trigger up to $13 billion in outflows, with active managers likely to cut exposure to avoid benchmarking mismatches. Massive liquidations risk severely weakening the rupiah and draining liquidity, compounding FX pressure as equities have already slumped more than 30% this year, making Indonesia the world’s worst-performing index in 2026. Selling was broad-based, led by financials, transport, non-cyclicals, healthcare, and property.

Corn Holds Near 2-Month Low — Corn futures held below $4.8 per bushel, staying near their lowest level in eight weeks, as expectations of a larger US crop and higher inventories outweighed the slower harvest pace. In its October outlook, the USDA raised its 2026/27 US corn yield estimate to 181.2 bushels per acre from 178.5 and projected production at 16.03 billion bushels, while ending stocks were forecast at 1.849 billion bushels. Meanwhile, the Crop Progress report showed 91% of the US corn crop had reached maturity as of October 11, while harvesting stood at 33% complete, six percentage points behind the five-year average. Crop conditions improved by 2 percentage points over the week, with 56% of the crop rated good to excellent. The USDA also raised its US export forecast by 25 million bushels to 3.3 billion, reflecting stronger demand and providing some support. However, the prospect of abundant supplies continued to overshadow export demand, keeping prices under pressure.

【藍佛安出席2026年世界銀行發展委員會第114次會議】10月15日，世界銀行（以下簡稱世行）在泰國曼谷舉行發展委員會第114次會議，審議透過了《私營資本動員和就業》報告、《2025年股權審議理事報告》。會議由發展委員會主席、瑞典財政部部長伊麗莎白·斯萬特松主持，來自世行25個選區的理事代表、世行行長彭安傑、國際貨幣基金組織總裁格奧爾基耶娃、世界貿易組織總幹事伊維拉等出席。財政部部長、世行中國理事藍佛安與會併發言，財政部副部長、世行中國副理事廖岷，部長助理常軍紅陪同參會。  藍佛安表示，當前國際形勢變亂交織，地緣衝突持續蔓延，全球經濟增長動能不足。世行作為全球最具影響力的多邊開發機構，應更好發揮逆週期作用，進一步提升發展融資能力，幫助成員國應對全球發展挑戰；加快構建全球資本動員平臺，創新發展融資工具，撬動更多私營資本，助力成員國私營部門發展；順應世界經濟格局變化，完善治理結構，解決部分成員股權嚴重低估問題，提升發展中成員代表性和發言權。中國是世行的主要股東國和重要發展夥伴，願與世行持續深化互利共贏合作，落實好新一期世行-中國國別夥伴框架，推動合作轉型，為全球減貧和發展事業提供更多公共產品。  藍佛安強調，今年以來，中國經濟頂住壓力穩健前行，呈現動能向新、結構向優的發展態勢，消費市場規模穩步擴大，進口規模實現較快增長，為不確定性增多的世界注入了寶貴的確定性。面向未來，中國將持續推動高質量發展，擴大高水平對外開放，落實全球發展倡議，同世界各國共享機遇、共創繁榮。

Offshore Yuan Holds at Strongest Since 2022 — The offshore yuan held around 6.69 per dollar on Friday, remaining at its strongest level since July 2022 as China accelerates efforts to establish the currency as a global player. Beijing is broadening the range of available investment products and financial instruments, including derivatives and hedging tools, while easing access to dim sum and panda bond issuance. Progress also includes expanding offshore-yuan investment options, deepening yuan-based financing, and strengthening cross-border clearing and settlement infrastructure. Meanwhile, caution lingered over persistent China-EU trade tensions as the bloc considers new powers to restrict access to its single market and assesses import data for potential surges, a preliminary step toward imposing quotas on additional goods. China has warned of retaliation against any new EU trade measures or discriminatory restrictions targeting Chinese companies or products beyond the hybrid vehicle export cap agreed last week.

Hong Kong Stocks Rebound as Asian Markets Recover — The Hang Seng Index rose 0.9%, or 212 points, to 24,272 on Friday, recovering from the previous session’s losses as Asian equities rebounded following a selloff in technology and semiconductor stocks, with dip buyers stepping in amid easing concerns over global market volatility. Lower yields helped ease pressure on equities, while investors bought beaten-down shares following recent market volatility and tracking gains in Wall Street. The recovery in regional chipmakers also helped lift risk appetite, although uncertainty surrounding the sustainability of artificial intelligence spending continued to weigh on parts of the technology sector. Investors also awaited Federal Reserve Chair Kevin Warsh’s comments for clues on the US interest-rate outlook. Hong Kong’s business confidence data was also due later in the day. Notable movers included Tencent (2.4%), Lenovo (1.7%), Xiaomi (1.8%), AIA (1.0%), and Z.AI Co. (3.3%).

【Guangdong yachts' southbound scheme launches, advancing Guangdong–Hong Kong–Macao yacht free travel】The Guangdong ‘yachts southbound’ scheme took effect today. On the morning of the 16th several Guangdong-registered yachts departed Guangzhou, Shenzhen and Zhuhai for Hong Kong waters. The move follows June’s implementation of northbound access for Hong Kong and Macau yachts and establishes two-way mutual access for yacht travel in the Guangdong–Hong Kong–Macao Greater Bay Area. Under the new rules eligible Guangdong-registered yachts may depart via six designated passenger terminals: Nansha Port passenger terminal (Nansha port area), Shekou Port passenger terminal, Fuyong passenger terminal at Shenzhen Airport port, Jiuzhou passenger terminal (Jiuzhou port area, Zhuhai), Guishan passenger terminal (Wanshan port area, Zhuhai) and Shengshi Yacht Club terminal (Shenwan port area, Zhongshan).

New Zealand Dollar Extends Gains — The New Zealand dollar rose to $0.562 on Friday, extending its rebound from an 18-month low, as the US dollar weakened amid falling Treasury yields. The greenback’s decline came despite strong inflation and spending data, which reinforced expectations that the Federal Reserve could continue raising interest rates. Hawkish remarks from RBNZ Chief Economist Paul Conway on Thursday also supported the kiwi. Conway said the late-October rate decision remains completely open and that the central bank continues to believe that an increase in the OCR is most likely, although the timing remains uncertain. Market attention now turns to next week’s third-quarter inflation data, which could provide fresh insight into the central bank’s policy direction. The report will be the final major data release before the RBNZ’s meeting on October 28. The kiwi has risen 0.3% this week, putting it on track to snap a seven-week losing streak.

China Stocks Rebound, Still Set for Weekly Decline — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.4% to 3,722 on Friday, while the Shenzhen Component gained 0.6% to 11,891, with both indices rebounding from more than one-year lows hit in the previous session as investors took a breather after a prolonged selloff. Still, concerns over renewed China-EU trade tensions persisted as the bloc weighs new powers to restrict access to its single market and assesses import data for potential surges, a first step toward imposing quotas on additional goods. China has warned of retaliation against new EU trade measures or discriminatory restrictions targeting its companies or products beyond the hybrid vehicle export cap agreed last week. AI-related stocks led the rebound as selling pressure eased, including Cambricon Technologies (1.4%), Hygon Information Technology (1.1%), Zhongji Innolight (2.2%), Eoptolink Technology (2%) and Victory Giant Technology (1.1%). For the week, both indices remained on track for a fourth consecutive weekly decline.

【香港：探討成立低空經濟產品出海示範區 支援與內地及國際的低空標準銜接】記者獲悉，香港發展低空經濟工作組與業內各方合作探討成立低空經濟產品出海示範區，旨在為低空經濟進出口貿易提供一站式配套服務，進一步支援國家的低空經濟企業出海。另外，國家在低空經濟的發展位居世界前列，在無人機制造、完整產業鏈和應用場景方面的發展尤其突出。由於現時國際尚未就統一低空技術標準達成共識，香港作為國際航空樞紐及國際創科中心，具備優越條件建立一個對標國際、接軌內地的低空經濟標準框架。就此，香港特區政府一直透過低空經濟“監管沙盒”及“監管沙盒X”驗證各項低空經濟技術，以健全本地低空經濟標準。香港特區政府積極推動與內地（尤其是粵港澳大灣區）及海外管轄區加強協作，支援本地與內地及國際的低空標準銜接。（人民財訊）

【科研人員發現單個神經元也能執行“智慧計算”】由中國科學家完成的研究顯示，單個神經元的樹狀分枝結構（樹突）就可以完成高階認知中的關鍵計算步驟。相關成果16日線上發表於國際學術期刊《科學》。團隊牽頭人、中國科學院腦科學與智慧技術卓越創新中心研究員徐寧龍介紹，這項研究更新了“單個神經元僅是認知計算中的簡單網路節點”這一傳統觀點，表明單個神經元內部即可完成支援靈活行為的認知計算，開闢了從神經細胞層面解析認知靈活性底層機制的研究正規化，有望啟發借鑑樹突多區室計算原理、設計更高效靈活的人工智慧系統。（新華社）

South Korean Shares Rebound After 7-Day Decline — The benchmark KOSPI rose more than 3% to around 5,730 on Friday, recovering part of a seven-day slide near an eleven-week low, as bargain hunting and a rebound in global equities lifted shares. Samsung Electronics and SK hynix climbed more than 2% and nearly 3%, respectively, while other notable gainers included SK Square (3.0%), Hyundai Motor (5.3%), Hanwha Aerospace (9.0%), Kia Corporation (4.9%), KB Financial Group (2.9%), and Doosan Enerbility (2.7%). Meanwhile, persistent concerns over AI valuations, Chinese competition in memory chips, and the sustainability of AI spending continued to weigh on the semiconductor sector, limiting the broader recovery. Additionally, hotter-than-expected US producer inflation reinforced concerns over tighter monetary conditions. At the same time, elevated oil prices amid escalating Middle East attacks and risks to regional energy supplies further heightened inflation risks.

### 2026-10-16 09:36:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Finds Support on Haven Demand — Silver held above $57 per ounce on Friday after rebounding in the previous session, as the recent selloff in equities drove safe-haven demand for government bonds and precious metals. The shift toward safer investments pushed bond yields sharply lower, easing pressure on non-yielding assets. Investors also weighed heightened geopolitical risks in the Middle East amid escalating hostilities between Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthis in Yemen, while a Singapore-flagged container ship was sunk in the Strait of Hormuz this week by an Iranian projectile. Meanwhile, market participants continued to evaluate the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy outlook, with stronger-than-expected US economic data reinforcing expectations of further interest rate increases. Markets are currently pricing in approximately a 72% probability that the Fed will leave rates unchanged this month, while the likelihood of a rate hike in December has climbed to 92%.

【庫克：AI應用正迎來爆發式增長 已顯著影響供應鏈】在今日舉辦的全球財富管理論壇2026上海蘇河灣大會上，蘋果公司執行董事長，清華大學經管學院顧問委員會主席蒂姆·庫克（Tim Cook）指出，人工智慧應用正迎來爆發式增長。當前，AI輔助程式設計顯著降低了程式設計門檻，使此前幾乎未接觸過程式碼的人群也能夠編寫程式碼。與此同時，人工智慧已在多個方面對供應鏈產生深遠影響。過去，質檢環節高度依賴人工。如今，機器學習使檢測變得高度自動化且更加精準。以iPhone顏色檢測為例，為確保數百萬臺量產裝置顏色保持一致，相關檢測與成像工作均藉助機器學習完成。

### 2026-10-16 09:24:54  #其他

特斯拉：如今路上行駛的每一輛車都受限於一點：輪胎抓地力。Roadster 的推進系統產生的推力是輪胎抓地力的 4-5 倍。在相同質量下施加更大的推力意味著顯著提升的加速效能——剩下的就交給物理定律了。

---

### 2026-10-16 09:24:02  #其他

特斯拉：SpaceX 幾十年來一直使用推進器來定位航天器。 讓它們在車輛中而不是真空環境中工作，需要全新的硬體和控制系統。 車內每個可用的空隙都塞滿了碳纖維纏繞壓力容器（COPVs） 。車上更多的壓縮氣體 = 更長的飛行時間。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價小幅回升，六款產品上漲，夢之藍M6+的大幅反彈對整體價盤形成支撐，但古20連續下挫也施加一定賣壓。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10749元，較昨日小幅上漲4元，結束前一日的回撥走勢。

### 2026-10-16 09:11:35  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK #EURUSD #Impact mixed #DXY #DAX

【World Economic Forum may appoint Lagarde as chair as early as November】Refinitiv cited sources saying the World Economic Forum could appoint ECB President Lagarde as its next chair as early as November, which would require her to step down from the ECB and prompt broader senior staffing changes. Lagarde's ECB term is scheduled to end in October next year; she has said she will remain into next year but has not committed to serving the full term. An early exit would allow EU leaders to pick a new ECB president before France's spring presidential election and could smooth agreement on appointments to two other senior ECB posts. Sources said the WEF board, where Lagarde is a member, meets on Nov. 2 and could elect her; the WEF aims to hold the handover by year-end. After Schnabel's resignation last month, Lagarde's departure would create a second vacancy among the ECB's six executive board members; ECB chief economist Philip Lane is due to leave in May next year.

### 2026-10-16 09:04:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Heating Oil Extends Losses — US heating oil futures fell below $4.80 per gallon, extending losses for a third consecutive session as prospects for increased fuel supplies weighed on prices. The IEA agreed to accelerate the release of remaining emergency oil stocks, including more than 20 million barrels of diesel. Meanwhile, Russia resumed diesel exports following a partial easing of restrictions, with a tanker carrying around 40,000 metric tons of diesel departing Primorsk for Morocco. These developments followed reports of a US proposal to ease sanctions on oil tankers linked to Russia's shadow fleet, potentially facilitating global oil flows. However, EIA data showed that US distillate inventories fell by 2.5 million barrels in the week ending October 9, highlighting persistent supply tightness. Attacks on shipping near the Strait of Hormuz and renewed strikes on Russian refineries also continued to threaten global fuel supplies.

【Myanmar refinery restarts to cut reliance on fuel imports】A long-idled refinery near Yangon resumed commercial operations on Wednesday for the first time in nearly a decade, part of Myanmar’s drive to reduce imports of gasoline and jet fuel. The Danlin refinery’s first phase is now online; a second phase is scheduled for completion in 2028. At full capacity the plant will process more than 60,000 barrels per day, meeting roughly half of Myanmar’s domestic fuel demand. President Min Aung Hlaing said securing adequate crude supply should be a priority.

### 2026-10-16 08:53:03  #其他

【沙特主導的多國聯軍通報軍事進展以及近期遭襲情況】沙特主導的多國聯軍15日召開沙特近期遭到葉門胡塞武裝襲擊以來的第一次新聞釋出會，通報聯軍軍事進展以及沙特國內近期遭襲情況。發言人表示，胡塞武裝對沙特利雅得機場的襲擊造成17人死亡和333人受傷。聯軍展示影片，顯示摧毀葉門境內胡塞武裝的導彈發射平臺和無人機等。發言人圖爾基·馬利基表示，胡塞武裝在區域各方的協助下獲得了先進技術，胡塞武裝利用部分民用設施和能源設施支援軍事行動，為導彈和無人機提供相關保障。沙特重申對葉門合法政府和武裝部隊的支援。馬利基表示，聯軍部隊近日摧毀了胡塞武裝在山區的127個倉庫設施以及眾多導彈發射裝置，以此削弱該民兵組織的軍事能力。馬利基表示，胡塞武裝應該立刻停止所有針對平民和民用區域，包括機場、港口、石油設施和基礎設施的恐怖襲擊行動。此前，該聯盟在一份宣告中指出，薩那的薩巴哈中央車站、地下燃料儲備、儲罐以及燃料運輸和解除安裝設施等地點正被用於軍事目的。

Singapore NODX Growth Hits Record High — Singapore’s non-oil domestic exports (NODX) soared 68.4% year-on-year in September 2026, accelerating sharply from a downwardly revised 46.1% increase in August and far exceeding forecasts of 33.8%. It marked the 23rd consecutive month of expansion and the strongest growth on record, mainly driven by electronics exports, which surged 107.6% compared with 13.6% in August, supported by robust AI-related demand. Electronic exports were boosted by disk media products (312.4%), PCs (149.5%), and integrated circuits (ICs) (74.7%). Meanwhile, non-electronic exports growth accelerated to 54.2% from an 11.9% increase in August.

### 2026-10-16 08:12:57  #MKT News

【Citic Securities flags opportunities in HDD, DRAM and NAND stocks】Citic Securities says global memory-chip stocks have become more volatile and have notably underperformed other sectors since October. Alongside macro headwinds — rising oil-price volatility and renewed inf and rate expectations — market attention has turned to progress on ChangXin Technology’s 4F² DRAM process. Investors fear a Chinese wafer‑fab breakthrough in high‑margin server DDR5 could weaken overseas suppliers’ capacity-based pricing power. DRAM remains dominated by 6F² cell architecture; deployment of 4F² depends on Samsung and ChangXin progress. 4F² is still in early validation; Citic flags yield, output, gross margins and downstream customer qualification as the key metrics to watch and judges near- to mid-term industry impact likely limited. The firm recommends focusing on investment opportunities in HDD, DRAM and NAND names.

【中信證券：建議關注HDD、DRAM、NAND賽道中公司的投資機會】中信證券研報稱，近期全球儲存晶片股價波動加劇，10月份以來表現顯著弱於其他板塊，除了宏觀因素的擾動外（原油價格波動上漲，通脹與利率預期重新抬頭），產業界對於長鑫科技4F²技術的進展亦變得更為關注。主要原因是市場擔心：中國本土晶圓廠在高毛利的伺服器DDR5市場形成有效技術破局，海外巨頭透過壟斷產能維持超高議價權的敘事邏輯會被動搖。從產業進展來看，目前DRAM的技術仍以6F²單元結構為主，4F²技術的落地需看三星電子和長鑫科技的進展。目前來看，4F²技術仍處於早期驗證階段，後續仍需觀察公司在這領域的良率、產量、毛利率、下游客戶認證情況等各個指標，對於中短期儲存產業的影響相對較小。建議關注HDD、DRAM、NAND賽道中公司的投資機會。

### 2026-10-16 07:52:42  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【CFTC 工作人員被要求撤回可疑石油交易分析報告】據報道，3 月 23 日，美國總統特朗普放棄打擊伊朗發電廠的威脅，這一訊息引發原油、股票及其他市場的劇烈買賣波動。

交易員早已習慣中東局勢訊息帶動價格大幅震盪，但那一次，部分交易者精準踩中時機。就在特朗普在 Truth 社交平臺發帖宣佈政策轉向前約 15 分鐘，市場出現價值數十億美元的合約押注：看跌原油、看漲標普 500 指數。此事引發廣泛猜測，懷疑有人獲取了不公平的資訊優勢。

美國商品期貨交易委員會（CFTC）負責調查該事件。這家聯邦衍生品監管機構，隨著加密貨幣與預測市場興起，監管範圍與受關注度大幅提升。委員會內一支資深取證分析師團隊按常規流程，整理當日市場盈虧交易資料。

知情人士稱（因涉及政府內部審議，要求匿名），市場監管部門的分析師根據觀測到的交易時點與交易主體資訊，向執法部門提交案件移交材料，由執法部門決定是否針對潛在內幕交易啟動全面調查。知情人士表示，兩個部門的代表還召開會議，研討最佳協作方式。

知情人士稱，接下來發生的事情極不尋常：由特朗普任命的 CFTC 主席邁克爾・塞利格的高階顧問梅爾・古納瓦德納，要求市場監管團隊的一名主管撤回這份案件移交材料。知情人士稱，該團隊當時不知所措。實操層面，他們甚至不清楚該如何執行這項要求，這類操作此前幾乎從未發生過。這件事也在另一方面打破慣例：以往一直由資深專業人員負責收集海量資料、撰寫初步報告這類專業且常規的工作，再由執法部門律師決定是否立案追查。知情人士表示，而如今，政治任命官員塞利格的一名顧問介入，意圖撤銷這份案件移交材料，並親自監督資料收集工作。古納瓦德納是華爾街資深人士，曾在特朗普第一任期任職於 CFTC；幾周前他重返該機構，擔任國際事務辦公室主管兼主席高階市場顧問，外界並不清楚他在本次證據蒐集流程中擁有何種職權。

### 2026-10-16 07:44:06  #公司

【多家銀行陸續贖回優先股】近日，江蘇銀行釋出擬贖回優先股的公告。今年以來，多家上市銀行陸續實施或披露優先股贖回計劃。記者梳理發現，今年以來，已有5家上市銀行完成優先股贖回，分別為光大銀行、平安銀行、招商銀行、北京銀行和南京銀行。其中，南京銀行贖回的“南銀優2”發行規模50億元，於9月完成贖回兌付。優先股密集贖回的背後，並不代表銀行資本補充壓力消失，行業資本補充需求仍將長期客觀存在。蘇商銀行特約研究員薛洪言認為，資本補充需求並未隨本輪贖回潮消退。息差收窄持續削弱銀行依靠利潤留存的內源資本補充能力，信貸投放、風險資產消耗以及附加資本監管要求，使資本佔用具備剛性，贖回省下的票息仍要服務於資本約束下的業務擴張。後續資本補充預計將以“贖舊發新”為主線，用永續債等替換存量高息資本工具。（證券日報）

【電子布價格漲勢延續 高階產能有望釋放】電子布價格本月延續上漲態勢，相關上市公司加快佈局低介電、低熱膨脹係數等高效能產品，透過新增產能與技術改造調整產品結構。國金證券10月9日釋出的研報顯示，本月以來，7628型號電子布價格再次上漲1.5元/米，G75電子紗上漲3000元/噸，2116和1080型號薄布均上漲2.5元/米。該研報認為，織布機供給緊張、下游需求超預期以及覆銅板價格持續上行等因素，共同推動了電子布價格的持續上漲。高效能電子布成為企業融資擴產的重點方向。科方得智庫研究負責人張新原對記者表示：“高階電子布織造對裝置精度、執行穩定性和生產效率要求較高，國產裝置在部分環節仍需持續改進，替代程序取決於技術攻關及產業鏈協作。由於裝置研發、製造和產線適配均需要時間，供給約束短期內可繼續支撐電子布價格。而隨著國產織布機技術進步和新增產能逐步釋放，供應緊張程度有望得到緩解，價格上漲趨勢可能放緩。”（證券日報）

【摩根大通、高盛上調 Volta AI 貸款收益率以吸引投資者】摩根大通與高盛集團上調 Volta Infrastructure Holdings Ltd. 一筆 50 億美元槓桿貸款的收益率並最佳化條款。當前投資者要求更高回報，才願意買入這隻繫結人工智慧熱潮的高風險債務。知情人士稱，兩家銀行自上週開始為這家 AI 雲初創企業 Volta 推介該筆融資，並於週四完成發售。這筆貸款收益率較基準利率上浮 6.5 個百分點，發行折價至 95 美分兌 1 美元面值。該定價使得收益率突破銀行此前預估的 11%，接近目前 AI 基建融資的最高利率水平。一位要求匿名（無權對外發言）的訊息人士透露，本次交易還增設更多投資者保護條款，並調整交易結構：若借款人提前償還債務，則需要支付罰金。摩根大通發言人拒絕置評，高盛與 Volta 方面未回應置評請求。Volta 屬於一批被稱為“新興雲服務商（neoclouds）”的 AI 公司，這類企業主要出租晶片及其他 AI 基礎設施的使用權。本次融資所得資金，將用於在挪威建設資料中心園區，由 Volta Infrastructure 與比特幣礦企、資料中心運營商 Bitdeer Technologies Group 合作運營。

### 2026-10-16 07:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

South Korea Jobless Rate Hits 2-Year Low — South Korea’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate fell to 2.6% in September 2026 from 2.7% in August, reaching its lowest level since September 2024. The economy added 113,000 jobs, marking three consecutive months of gains over 100,000 and bringing total employment to 29.27 million. Human health and social work activities led job creation, adding 207,000 workers, while manufacturing rose by 16,000, its first increase since June 2024 and ending a 26-month streak of declines. In contrast, accommodation and food service employment fell by 97,000 for a second consecutive month, recording its largest decline since February 2021. Employment also fell in agriculture, forestry and fishing (-94,000) and construction (-48,000). Meanwhile, the number of unemployed people was 600,000, down 34,000 from the same month a year ago, while the economically inactive population increased by 179,000 to 16,188,000. Youth employment rate for those aged 15 to 29 was 44.0%, down 1.1 percentage points.

【TCW將信用風險敞口降至30年來最低 警告CCC級借款人會最先撐不住】面對估值和風險蔓延方面的擔憂，TCW規模1600億美元的固定收益團隊已將投資組合的信用風險敞口降至30年來最低水平。該團隊首席投資官Bryan Whalen在彭博行業研究Credit Edge播客節目中表示，“回顧過去30年 —— 我們對投資級債券和高收益債券的敞口從未像現在這麼低過。”由於市場擔心債務成本上升將迫使更多高槓杆企業違約，CCC評級債券自9月底以來一直處於困境債券水平。與此同時，質量較高的債券利差相對穩定。“這是最弱的借款人，他們會最先撐不住，”Whalen在談到CCC評級債券時表示，“毫無疑問，接下來單B評級借款人會感受到壓力，然後就是雙B評級借款人。”

### 2026-10-16 06:59:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Poised for Second Straight Weekly Gain — The NZX 50 climbed 24 points, or 0.2%, to 13,857 in Friday morning trade, extending gains from the previous two sessions to reach its highest level since September 22. The index tracked a rise on Wall Street overnight as the tech selloff faded and Treasury yields eased. Fresh data also lifted sentiment, as New Zealand's factory activity growth accelerated in August. However, food inflation hit a three-month high, while electronic card spending continued to fall in September, capping the gains. Traders also looked ahead to the release of Q3 inflation and trade data, along with a slew of Chinese data, including GDP growth, due next week. Healthcare, consumer discretionary, and consumer staples stocks mainly supported the index, with notable gains from Fisher & Paykel (1.1%), South Port of New Zealand (1.1%), Infratil (1.0%), and EBOS Group (0.5%). For the week, the index is heading for a 0.8% rise, which would mark its second straight weekly increase.

### 2026-10-16 06:37:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Credit Card Spending #Importance 1

New Zealand Electronic Card Spending Falls 0.4% in September — Seasonally adjusted electronic card transactions in New Zealand fell 0.4% month-on-month in September 2026, following a 0.9% drop in the previous month. The smaller decline came as spending on consumables (0.3% vs -0.4% in August) and motor vehicles (0.6% vs -0.3%) both rebounded, while spending on fuel rose at a faster pace (2.9% vs 1.3%). These gains were offset by declines in spending on hospitality (-2.3% vs -1.9% in August), durables (-0.5% vs -1.6%), and apparel (-2.0% vs -2.0%). On an annual basis, total electronic card spending rose 0.9%, accelerating from a 0.2% gain in August, which was the softest increase since December 2025.

### 2026-10-16 06:33:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Food Inflation #Importance 1

New Zealand Food Inflation at 3-Month High of 2.4% — Annual food inflation in New Zealand accelerated to 2.4% in September 2026 from 1.9% in the previous month, which was the softest increase since December 2024. It was the fastest increase in food inflation since June, as inflation for grocery food prices accelerated to 3.0% from 1.7% in August, making it the largest contributor to the annual increase in food prices. Inflation for restaurant meals and ready-to-eat food eased slightly to 3.1% from 3.2%, making it the second-largest contributor to the annual increase in food prices. Meanwhile, prices for meat, poultry, and fish increased by 3.2%, compared with 3.4% in August, while fruit and vegetable prices fell at a faster rate, declining 1.8% following a 1.6% drop in August.

【美聯儲哈馬克：接二連三的供應衝擊可能引發通脹思維】克利夫蘭聯邦儲備銀行行長貝絲·哈馬克表示，她擔心接二連三的供應衝擊可能會導致企業形成通脹思維，從而加大政策制定者遏制價格壓力的難度。“企業正感受到很大的投入成本壓力。他們看到價格繼續以比過去習慣的速度更快地上漲，”哈馬克在紐約由高盛主辦的一場活動上表示。“我擔心的一件事是，由於我們經歷了這些接連不斷的供應衝擊，到了某個時候，企業會開始形成一種預期，認為還會有更多此類衝擊到來，而且價格只會繼續上漲，”哈馬克表示。“如果形成了這些價格壓力會變得越來越持久的預期，那麼貨幣政策要完成我們需要做的工作就會困難得多。與沒有形成通脹思維的情況相比，我們可能需要採取幅度更大的行動，”哈馬克表示。

### 2026-10-16 05:43:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

New Zealand Manufacturing Strengthens in September — New Zealand’s BusinessNZ Performance of Manufacturing Index rose to 56.5 in September 2026 from 53.2 in August, signaling a stronger expansion in manufacturing activity. Employment was the weakest sub-index at 51.8, although it remained in expansion and improved from 50 in August. Finished Stocks (59) and Deliveries (58.8) led the gains, while Production and New Orders both rose to 57. Despite the stronger reading, sentiment remained mixed, with 53% of comments being negative. Respondents continued to cite high fuel prices and uncertainty ahead of the election, although others pointed to seasonal activity, pre-Christmas orders, and improving demand. Overall, strength in New Orders, Production, and Finished Stocks suggests manufacturing momentum is building heading into the final quarter of the year.

### 2026-10-16 05:17:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Import Prices YoY #Importance 1

South Korea Import Prices Rise in September — South Korea’s import prices rose 17.6% year-over-year in September 2026, accelerating from a 15.6% increase in August. Price gains were led by raw materials, which climbed 39.3%, while intermediate goods prices rose 14.3%. In contrast, capital goods prices fell 1.5% and consumer goods prices edged down 0.4%. By economic sector, mining product prices surged 43.6%. Manufactured goods prices rose 9%, led by a 44.4% increase in coal and petroleum products. Meanwhile, prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products increased 2.7%. The Import Price Index rose 1.7% month-over-month. The Import Volume Index increased 11.1% year-over-year, while the Net Barter Terms of Trade Index rose 25.4%.

### 2026-10-16 04:33:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Slides Following Election Runoff Polls — The Ibovespa fell 1.1% to close at 204,324 on Thursday as investors reassessed the political outlook ahead of Brazil’s presidential runoff later this month. New polls showed Flávio Bolsonaro’s lead over President Lula narrowing, tempering optimism that had fueled a rally in Brazilian assets last week after the senator unexpectedly finished ahead of Lula in the first round. Although Flávio Bolsonaro has yet to present detailed policy plans, investors view him as more likely to pursue fiscal reforms, including spending cuts, which are seen as essential to lowering interest rates. However, questions remain about the fiscal discipline of a potential Flávio administration. Political pressure has already prompted the candidate to change his position and support ending the six-days-on, one-day-off work schedule, which the Senate approved yesterday. Among the notable laggards, Itaú fell 1.3%, Banco do Brasil gained 1.8%, Axia and Petrobras each lost 1.3%, and WEG dropped 2.7%.

TSX Rises as Banks and Tech Stocks Rebound — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 1.5% to close at 36,137 on Thursday as banking and technology stocks rebounded sharply from earlier losses. Lower global bond yields boosted risk appetite after a selloff in Wall Street chipmakers drove investors toward safe-haven assets such as government bonds. Canada’s benchmark 10-year bond yield fell about 8 basis points, supporting broad-based gains across sectors. Interest-rate-sensitive stocks advanced, with RBC up 1.1%, TD Bank gaining 1.5%, and National Bank rising 1.9%. Retailers also gained, with Loblaw jumping 5% and Dollarama advancing 2.7%. Meanwhile, the selloff in chipmakers slowed sharply, while US hyperscalers mostly closed higher, lending support to Canadian technology stocks. Shopify rose 2.9%, Constellation Software jumped 4.6%. ### 2026-10-16 04:08:16  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Rebound as Chipmaker Selloff Eases — US stock indices closed higher on Thursday, rebounding from earlier losses as the selloff in AI infrastructure stocks eased. The S&P 500 gained 0.3%, the Nasdaq closed near flat, and the Dow added 557 points. Chipmakers and AI hyperscalers pared losses after Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang pushed back against concerns about CoreWeave (+5.9%), saying he did not believe the company would need a bailout from Nvidia. The data center firm is an important test case for the viability of businesses emerging to meet surging demand for AI computing. Companies like CoreWeave have borrowed heavily with support from suppliers, including Nvidia (-1.8%), fueling concerns that circular financing may be creating artificial demand. Most hyperscalers closed higher, with Microsoft up 1.1% and Oracle gaining 3%. The chip selloff slowed sharply, with Broadcom down 0.6%, AMD losing 1.5%, while Micron rose 1.9%. The Dow was supported by a rally in Treasuries after a rout that had propelled yields to multi-year highs.

Brent Rises as Middle East Tensions Escalate — Brent crude rose near $106 a barrel on Thursday amid mounting supply risks in the Middle East. Attacks on commercial vessels in the Strait of Hormuz sank a Singapore-flagged container ship, pushing tanker rates in the Persian Gulf to record highs. The US military has reportedly drawn up plans for new strikes on Iran, potentially involving the MOAB, the largest non-nuclear explosive device in the US arsenal. Iran said it was “counting the moments” until it could strike US warships. Prices were also supported by an 8-million-barrel US inventory draw in the week ended October 9th. The IEA warned that tight diesel markets could persist into next year, while attacks on Saudi Arabia threatened to disrupt the recovery in oil supply. Meanwhile, the US is reportedly considering easing sanctions on “shadow fleet” vessels transporting Russian oil and petroleum products to help ease global supply pressures.

Paraguay Consumer Confidence Weakens in September — Paraguay’s Consumer Confidence Index fell to 39.09 points in September 2026 from 39.67 in August, marking its lowest reading since May. The Economic Situation Index declined by 1.50 points month-over-month to 28.42, while the Economic Expectations Index rose by 0.33 points to 49.75. Regarding durable goods purchases, month-over-month increases were recorded for cars and motorcycles, while the housing category remained unchanged and purchases of household appliances declined.

### 2026-10-16 03:26:32  #公司 #市場

【華爾街資深人士稱布倫特原油淪為“情緒合約” 已與現實世界脫節】華爾街資深人士Jeff Currie表示，全球最重要的石油基準期貨如今幾乎已經淪為一種“情緒合約”，削弱了其作為價格波動管理工具的實用性。“所有與能源相關的東西都在上漲，唯獨布倫特原油沒有，”Currie週四接受採訪時表示。“它已經與現實世界脫節了。”Currie是投資公司Real Macro的創始人兼執行長。他表示，布倫特原油目前約每桶106美元的價格，並未反映出從霍爾木茲海峽到黑海一帶供應所面臨威脅的程度。近期石油市場面臨的威脅進一步升級，霍爾木茲海峽一帶針對油輪的襲擊頻發，胡塞武裝對沙烏地阿拉伯的襲擊愈演愈烈。運費市場也出現飆升。Currie表示，隨著全球環境惡化，布倫特原油與現貨市場的脫節正在造成“無法定價的風險”，使對沖者在最需要利用這一合約保護自身之際，反而不願使用它。“這些期貨市場對於現代社會中一個行業的運轉絕對至關重要，”Currie表示。“如果失去這種定價機制，就不會有足夠的投資，也不會有足夠的行為調整。”
